app=oo-pg-source user=lot_reader sql=SELECT pg_catalog.pg_is_in_recovery(), pg_catalog.pg_postmaster_start_time()::text, pg_catalog.current_database()::text, SESSION_USER::text, CURRENT_USER::text, w.ord::int4, c.oid::int8, c.relkind::text, a.attnum::int4, a.attname::text, a.atttypid::int8, a.atttypmod::int4, a.attnotnull, pk.attnums::int4[], pk.names::text[] FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[]) WITH ORDINALITY AS w(schema_name, table_name, ord) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.nspname = w.schema_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_class c ON c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relname = w.table_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.oid AND a.attnum > 0 AND NOT a.attisdropped LEFT JOIN LATERAL ( SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attnum::int4 ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::int4[]) AS attnums, COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attname::text ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::text[]) AS names FROM pg_catalog.pg_index i JOIN LATERAL unnest(i.indkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS u(attnum, ord) ON true LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a2 ON a2.attrelid = c.oid AND a2.attnum = u.attnum AND a2.attnum > 0 AND NOT a2.attisdropped WHERE i.indrelid = c.oid AND i.indisprimary ) pk ON true ORDER BY w.ord, a.attnum NULLS FIRST LIMIT $3

app=oo-pg-source user=lot_reader sql=SELECT pg_catalog.pg_is_in_recovery(), pg_catalog.pg_postmaster_start_time()::text, pg_catalog.current_database()::text, SESSION_USER::text, CURRENT_USER::text, w.ord::int4, c.oid::int8, c.relkind::text, a.attnum::int4, a.attname::text, a.atttypid::int8, a.atttypmod::int4, a.attnotnull, pk.attnums::int4[], pk.names::text[] FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[]) WITH ORDINALITY AS w(schema_name, table_name, ord) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.nspname = w.schema_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_class c ON c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relname = w.table_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.oid AND a.attnum > 0 AND NOT a.attisdropped LEFT JOIN LATERAL ( SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attnum::int4 ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::int4[]) AS attnums, COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attname::text ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::text[]) AS names FROM pg_catalog.pg_index i JOIN LATERAL unnest(i.indkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS u(attnum, ord) ON true LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a2 ON a2.attrelid = c.oid AND a2.attnum = u.attnum AND a2.attnum > 0 AND NOT a2.attisdropped WHERE i.indrelid = c.oid AND i.indisprimary ) pk ON true ORDER BY w.ord, a.attnum NULLS FIRST LIMIT $3

app=oo-a125-admin user=postgres sql=ALTER TABLE drift.item OWNER TO lot_writer
app=oo-a125-admin user=postgres sql=SELECT c.oid::int8 FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace WHERE n.nspname = $1 AND c.relname = $2

app=oo-pg-source user=lot_reader sql=SELECT c.oid::int8, c.relkind::text, c.relrowsecurity, c.relforcerowsecurity, pg_catalog.has_table_privilege(CURRENT_USER, c.oid, 'SELECT'), pg_catalog.has_table_privilege(CURRENT_USER, c.oid, 'INSERT'), pg_catalog.has_table_privilege(CURRENT_USER, c.oid, 'UPDATE'), pg_catalog.has_table_privilege(CURRENT_USER, c.oid, 'DELETE'), pg_catalog.has_table_privilege(CURRENT_USER, c.oid, 'TRUNCATE') FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace WHERE n.nspname = $1 AND c.relname = $2
app=oo-pg-source user=lot_reader sql=SELECT a.attnum::int4, a.attname::text, a.atttypid::int8, a.atttypmod, a.attnotnull, pg_catalog.has_column_privilege(CURRENT_USER, a.attrelid, a.attnum, 'SELECT') FROM pg_catalog.pg_class c JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.oid AND a.attnum > 0 AND NOT a.attisdropped WHERE n.nspname = $1 AND c.relname = $2 ORDER BY a.attnum
app=oo-pg-source user=lot_reader sql=SELECT pg_catalog.pg_is_in_recovery(), pg_catalog.pg_postmaster_start_time()::text, pg_catalog.current_database()::text, SESSION_USER::text, CURRENT_USER::text, w.ord::int4, c.oid::int8, c.relkind::text, a.attnum::int4, a.attname::text, a.atttypid::int8, a.atttypmod::int4, a.attnotnull, pk.attnums::int4[], pk.names::text[] FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::text[]) WITH ORDINALITY AS w(schema_name, table_name, ord) LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_namespace n ON n.nspname = w.schema_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_class c ON c.relnamespace = n.oid AND c.relname = w.table_name LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a ON a.attrelid = c.oid AND a.attnum > 0 AND NOT a.attisdropped LEFT JOIN LATERAL ( SELECT COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attnum::int4 ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::int4[]) AS attnums, COALESCE(array_agg(a2.attname::text ORDER BY u.ord), '{}'::text[]) AS names FROM pg_catalog.pg_index i JOIN LATERAL unnest(i.indkey) WITH ORDINALITY AS u(attnum, ord) ON true LEFT JOIN pg_catalog.pg_attribute a2 ON a2.attrelid = c.oid AND a2.attnum = u.attnum AND a2.attnum > 0 AND NOT a2.attisdropped WHERE i.indrelid = c.oid AND i.indisprimary ) pk ON true ORDER BY w.ord, a.attnum NULLS FIRST LIMIT $3
